Macaulay2 » Documentation
Packages » SimplicialModules :: SimplicialModule _ Array
next | previous | forward | backward | up | index | toc

SimplicialModule _ Array -- the canonical inclusion or projection map of a direct sum

  • Operator: _
  • Usage:
    i = C_[name]
    p = C^[name]
  • Inputs:
    • C, a Simplicial Module,
    • name, an array,
  • Outputs:
    • a Map of Simplicial Modules, i is the canonical inclusion and p is the canonical projection

Description

The direct sum is an n-ary operator with projection and inclusion maps from each component satisfying appropriate identities.

One can access these maps as follows.

i1 : S = ZZ/101[a,b,c];
 
i2 : C1 = simplicialModule(freeResolution coker vars S, 5, Degeneracy => true)

      1      4      10      20      35      56
o2 = S  <-- S  <-- S   <-- S   <-- S   <-- S  <-- ...
                                            
     0      1      2       3       4       5

o2 : SimplicialModule
 
i3 : C2 = simplicialModule(complex (ideal(a,b,c)) , 5, Degeneracy => true)

o3 = image | a b c | <-- image | a b c | <-- image | a b c | <-- image | a b c | <-- image | a b c | <-- image | a b c |<-- ...
                                                                                                          
     0                   1                   2                   3                   4                   5

o3 : SimplicialModule
